**D99 in the reference** (decided by the lead: a borrow-returning call that runs returns the fresh borrow it reserves): `runCallRef` reserves the result's label when the codomain is a borrow, lends it where the borrow arguments lent, runs the body in place, and renames the returned borrow into it (`SState.endInto`). `Regress/CallInPlaceD99.id1_inPlace_typed` re-checks `Id1(&y)` in place against `callOf` under it (kernel-checked). `callOf_inPlace` is restated against it: the same result, its label included, and the same renaming; `callOf_inPlace_rel` is its form at a call related to the checker's, for every body (the `Id` hypotheses discharged from the checker's run: D98, typing).

**The assemblies** (`Steps/CallAsm.lean`; no `sorry` in their bodies, each leaf a named `sorry` with its one-paragraph statement): `runBody_step` (front, the body by the induction hypothesis in tail position, back, the run in place re-read under the final assignment), `nestedRun_sim` (the nested start related to the empty state by `Rel.start`, the step at every future, one assignment for all), `callFn_step` (front with the reservation, the call proper by `closeCall_step` or `runBody_step`, the tail), `closeCall_step` (checks, the fills by induction one at a time, the result). They are now the proofs of `Call.runBody_step`, `callFn_step`, `closeCall_step` and `nestedRun_sim`.

**Found by writing them** (all fixed in the open):
1. *The induction hypothesis ran every term out of tail position.* `eval_step_stmt`, `evalCore_step_stmt` and `letIn_step_stmt` compared the checker with `run` (`runT false`), while a body runs in tail position on both sides (`runBody` sets `tailNext`; `runBodyRef` runs `runT true`). Now the reference's tail flag is the checker state's (`runT … st.tailNext`). Backed by the assembly: the body's step has no other hypothesis to use.
2. *The simulation form kept the context and concluded no `CtxOk`.* `SimConcl`, `SimConclT`, `eval_step_stmt`, `evalCore_step_stmt`, `letIn_step_stmt` and `nfSealed_step_stmt` (with `nestedRun_sim`) now conclude in the extended form the join's statements already had (`Δ` extended at new unknowns, `CtxOk` after, the tables only grow): without it no step could follow another (the pop after a body).
3. *The value a step returns was not counted.* The same statements related the checker's state without the returned value; the reference parks a returned borrow in a temporary (D14), and the relation matches temporaries by the borrows they hold, so after `&x` nothing matched (`Regress/InFlight.not_rel_without_flight`, kernel-checked: the reference's state after `&y` is related to no checker state whose frame holds no borrow in a temporary, which is the checker's after its `borrowPlace`). Now the checker's state is read with the value in flight (`MState.withTemp`, `GoodF` with `flightOf`), as the borrow statements already did.
4. *A body runs at one fuel less than the hypothesis.* `runBody` at `fuel + 1` evaluates its body at `fuel - 1`, the induction hypothesis is at `fuel`. Decided: strong induction in the knot (`StepsIHBelow`, `Knot.stepsIH_below`); the hypothesis is at every fuel up to the step's.
5. *The nested run needs one assignment for every future* (`nested_uniform`, a leaf): the induction hypothesis gives an assignment per future, the statement one for all; a loan-free text run from an empty frame reads the future only at labels it makes, so the values of the unknowns it creates do not depend on it.
6. *`callFn`'s hypotheses must supply [Close]'s readings and the run in place's success* (`callFn_front`): `closeCall_step_stmt` needs the row, the declared-`Unit` readings and the cells' types tied to the code, `runBody_step_stmt` that the run in place succeeds; `callFn_step_stmt` has neither, so they are the front's obligations (the decisions' alignment; the callee's meaning a member of its type).

**The rule for every step statement from now on** (the lead's decision, 2026-11-20): a statement in
the simulation form concludes in the extended form and counts the value it returns as in flight:
an assignment and a `Δ` extending the old ones at the unknowns the step created
(`∀ σ < st.absTy.size, ρ' σ = ρ σ ∧ Δ' σ = K.Δ σ`), a renaming agreeing on the labels issued, the
tables only growing (`st.absTy.size ≤ st'.absTy.size`, `st.nextLoan ≤ st'.nextLoan`), `CtxOk` of the
state after under the extended context, the relation and `GoodF` of the state after **with the
returned value counted** (`st'.withTemp v`, `flightOf r.2`). `SimConcl` and `SimConclT` are that
form; the remaining statements in the old form are migrated with their areas, and no new statement is
written in it.

**The decisions are extended with `Δ`** (decided 2026-11-20, after the slice prover's finding that
`access_ctx` is false: an access can grow the table of unknowns, an end's [Repack] making the lent
part a fresh unknown, and the context's decisions, fixed, cannot agree with the checker's on inputs
naming it). The extended context is `{ K with Δ := Δ', D := D' }`, with a new decision table `D'`
that agrees with the old one on every input naming only unknowns that existed before the step,
`K.D.AgreeOld st.absTy.size D'` (`Decisions.AgreeOld`: places and readings name no unknown and agree
outright; a term's decision agrees when its unknowns are below the bound; `refl`, `mono`, `trans`).
The other form offered (keep `D`, restrict `CtxOk`'s decision clause to inputs over existing
unknowns, the extension a separate fact) was not taken: it would weaken `CtxOk` itself, so every
reader of the decisions would carry a side condition on the unknowns of its input, while extending
`D` with `Δ` is the left half of D57/D65 completed in the one place the context grows. The old
inputs keep their decisions (`decAligned_grow`, and `runT_congrD` for a run that reads only old
inputs). Applied to every statement in the extended form (`SimConcl`, `SimConclT`, `eval`,
`evalCore`, `letIn`, `nfSealed`, `observe`, `idType`, the call statements and their assemblies,
the chain's `const_value`); `movedPlace`, `dropOne` and the join's drop get it when the slice
branch is merged. The induction hypothesis is at every fuel up to the step's (`StepsIHBelow`, the knot by
strong induction: the lead's decision on item 4 below, in place of a fuel-monotonicity fact about the
checker).

**Running count, per area** (for the lead's tracking in docs/32):

| Area | Checker faults | Model definition changes | Relation changes | Statements found false |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Calls (step 1, D69–D71) | 1 (D69: a running call kept the argument's label; D99, a checker change) | 1 (`runCallRef`: lent too little, then D99's reservation and renaming) | 0 to `Rel` (the frame relation is new, for calls) | 4 (part (a)'s renaming at `IdP`; the tail flag; the extended context; the value in flight) |

### D72. The top-level chain assembled; the knot of [Def] (fifth lead, 2026-11-20; area 2a)

**Assembled, no `sorry` in the bodies** (`Meaning/Consistency.lean`): `consistencyBounded_assembled` (from `consistency_rel` and the existence of a data model, resolution being a function of the program), `consistency_rel` (a parameterless definition of type `False` would mean a member of `prop False`, a proof of `False`), `defn_member` (by induction on the items, one at a time), `step_member` (an inductive declaration adds no definition; a definition is `step_member_const` or `step_member_fix`), and `step_member_const`: the check taken apart (`const_split`), the body by `eval_step` from the start related to the empty state, [Return]'s membership by `bodyReturns_sound` in the extended context with the value in flight, the constant's meaning that value's (`const_value`). The induction hypothesis there is the knot's (`Knot.stepsIH`, every fuel). The data model's faithfulness (the data-model lane's finding 1) is a named hypothesis of the chain, `DataFaithful`, an opaque placeholder until that lane's statement replaces it and enters `CtxOk`; `exists_dataModel` now owes it at every prefix.

**The knot of [Def]: the chain cannot be closed for a function** (`Regress/DefKnot.ctx_contains_fix_member`, kernel-checked). `checkDef` pushes a function's global before it runs `checkFix`, so that recursive calls find it. `checkFix_sound_stmt` asks `CtxOk` at that state, and `CtxOk`'s `GlobalsOk` asks every global of the state to mean the closure of its code (`fn`), at its stored type (`ty`), and to be a member of it (`mem`): for the global being defined that is `checkFix_sound`'s conclusion. So `checkFix_sound` is vacuous at a definition, and no statement proves an accepted function a member of its type (`step_member_fix`, the leaf the chain stops at). Every step lemma run inside the body assumes the same `CtxOk`, so the circularity is not local to `checkFix_sound`. What breaks it: the guarded recursion on the decreasing argument, by induction on its rank: `CtxOk` asks of the definition being checked only what the recursion gives, membership at arguments of smaller rank (or its meaning the stage before, `RunCongr.fixStage`, with that stage's membership), and `checkFix_sound` is proved by induction on the rank bound. That changes `GlobalsOk` for the definition being checked; not done, for the lead. The rank itself is changing (the data-model lane's finding 2: rank as the height of the value's meaning), so its form waits on that port.

**Also found by assembling**: `bodyReturns_sound_stmt` asked `Good` of the state without the value it checks; after the in-flight rule (D71) the step before it gives `GoodF` of the state with the value counted, and `Good` of the state without it is false when the value is a borrow (its loan has no borrow in the state). Restated with the value in flight (`GoodF … (st.withTemp v) fl`, its typed future `TypedFutureF`); the constant's assembly is what uses it.

**Open leaves of the chain**: `accepted_resolves`, `resolves_of_accepted`, `resolvesTo_unique`, `covered_prefix` (checker facts about the program loop); `falseConst_ty`, `false_means_empty`; `exists_dataModel` (now with faithfulness); `const_split`, `typedFutureF_exists`, `const_value`; `step_member_fix` (the knot).

| Area | Checker faults | Model definition changes | Relation changes | Statements found false |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Top-level chain (2a, D72) | 0 | 0 | 0 to `Rel`; one to `CtxOk` proposed (`GlobalsOk` for the definition being checked) | 2 (`checkFix_sound` vacuous at a definition; `bodyReturns_sound` without the value in flight) |

### D73. The knot of [Def]: design note (fifth lead, 2026-11-20; paper only, built after the data-layer port)

The plan's outer induction, on the size of the decreasing argument, missing from the Lean statements (D72). What changes, and what the rank must provide.

**1. The clause for the definition being checked.** The run context names the definition being checked and a bound: `RunCtx.cur : Option (String × Nat)` (its name and `b`). `GlobalsOk` asks of every other global what it asks now (`fn`, `ty`, `mem`). Of the global named by `cur` it asks `fn` and `ty` as now (its meaning is the closure of its code, its full guarded fixpoint, at its stored type) and, in place of `mem`, membership BELOW the bound: `MemBelow b f T`, the closure applied at any member arguments whose decreasing component has rank below `b` is defined, its result a member of the codomain at them and its finals members of the borrow parameters' types. A non-recursive definition (`dec = none`) is asked nothing at `cur` (its body never calls itself, [Rec]). `cur` enters at `checkDef`'s push of the global; before it, and for an earlier global, nothing changes. The full closure, not a stage, is the global's meaning: the stages appear only inside the proof (below). A closure whose self binding holds the closure being checked (a local `fix`) is the same with `Membership` in place of `GlobalsOk` (item 5).

**2. `checkFix_sound` by induction on the bound.** `P(b)`: `MemBelow b g Π` for the definition's closure `g`. `P(0)` is vacuous. `P(b) → P(b + 1)`: take member arguments whose decreasing component has rank at most `b`; the assignment of the generic state that gives each parameter's unknown its argument is typed (`generic_good`); `CtxOk` holds at the generic state under `cur := (f, b)` (earlier globals by the chain's induction, `f` itself by `P(b)`); the generic run (`Rel.start`, `checkTail_step` along the case tree, the return check by `conv_sound` and `bodyReturns_sound`) gives a leaf whose value is a member of the goal and is the reference's run of the body in the closure's frame at those arguments (the frame property, D70, with `self` the closure). That run is `g` at the arguments, because `g args = stage (rank args + 1) args` and every call it makes to itself is at a smaller rank (item 3), where `g` and the stage agree. The full meaning: every argument's decreasing component has some finite rank `r`, and `P(r + 1)` covers it; so `g` is defined and a member at every member argument, `mkTele` succeeds, and the closure is a member of its Π-type. `recCheck_sound_stmt` (which states the whole membership on the closure value) is replaced by this induction and the call-step statement of item 3.

**3. A recursive call at the call step.** Under `cur := (f, b)`, a recursive call (through `self` or the global) passes a decreasing argument that [Rec] (`recCheck`, run by `callFn` in the typed generic run) accepted as a STRICT PART of the entry value: a field of a field … of the parameter's value as it was passed, through matches, `let`s and borrows. A new statement supplies its meaning: `recDecreases_stmt`, from a related state, a successful `recCheck` means the decreasing argument's meaning is a strict part of the entry argument's meaning, under the assignment and at every typed future. With the rank's property below, its rank is below the entry's, hence below `b`, so `MemBelow b` gives the callee's membership there (what `runBody_step`'s `hok` and `callFn_front` need: the run in place succeeds and is `callOf`). **What the rank must satisfy** (for the data-model lane): (R1) it is defined on every meaning (whole, partly moved, open: the entry value at a recursive call is often partly moved, `match x { Cons(h, t) => f(t) }`); (R2) a strict part has strictly smaller rank, through any nesting (fields of fields; through a nested declaration's parameters, a forest inside a tree: the finding at `List(Rose(Nat))`), whatever the parts moved out of the whole; (R3) a member's rank is its element's (the run decodes and re-encodes members: `callOf` passes the arguments' meanings, `bodyAt` decodes them); (R4) the entry's rank is taken at the call's start, the argument's when it is passed, and (R2) relates them (a part moved out of the entry before the recursive call is still a part of the entry as it was passed).

**4. Which statements change.** Every statement that assumes `CtxOk` changes its hypothesis (all of `StepsIH`), since `GlobalsOk` is part of it, and `RunCtx` gains `cur`. Proofs that could notice: those that read a global's membership or meaning: the call steps (`callFn` on a `.gfn` or `self` callee: `fres`, `hok`, `callOf_inPlace_rel`; `runBody` through `fixOf`), `evalCore`'s `.const` (reads `K.C.global`), `funType`/`valType` of a global, `recCheck`, and `conv` where it compares function values. Proofs that cannot: the drop at the join and the leaves under it (they read places, sites and declared readings, never a global's membership), the borrow steps, [End], [Access], substitution. The slice prover's leaves (`dataMean_ne_proof`, `access_root_not_proof`, `access_ctx`, `copyRead_aligned`, `runs_valTypeDecl_keep`) read no global: their statements change only in carrying the new `CtxOk`, their proofs not at all.

**5. Local closures and function parameters.** A local `fix` (a closure formed in a body): `checkFix` at the fix site runs its body generically with its captures; its `self` binding holds the closure value, whose meaning is the full closure (`meanV`'s `.clo`, the guarded fixpoint by stages). `Good`'s `Membership` asks that binding to be a member of its Π-type, the same circularity as `GlobalsOk.mem`; the same fix: the self binding of the closure being checked is asked `MemBelow b`, the bound in the context beside `cur`, and the induction of item 2 proves its membership. A non-recursive local closure needs no bound. A closure value met later (captured, passed, returned) is a member by the step that formed it (`capture_step`, the `.fix` clause's reading), not by a new induction. A function parameter (an unknown of a Π-type, or a borrow of one): its meaning is the assignment's, a member by `TypedAssignment`; nothing to prove and no recursion through it.

The [Obs-ref] case at `Unit` and `Nat` is `Regress/IdUnitWrite.lean` (D26).

### D75. Equality and `J`: the statements, the assemblies, three statements found false (fifth lead, 2026-11-20; area (e))

**Statements in the final form.** `eq_step_stmt` and `J_step_stmt` are in the simulation form with the type (`SimConclT`), from a related state, with `hok` (the restriction of untyped runs, as `evalCore_step_stmt`). The private computations (`mkEqM`, `conv`, `convPi`, `convFn`, `evalType`) conclude `KeptAfter` (Ω, goal, path condition and declarations the snapshot's; the tables only grow, old types kept) and `ExtOk` (an extension of the assignment, `Δ` and the decisions under which the state after meets `CtxOk` and is good). `mkEqM` now says its value MEANS the proposition `⟦a⟧ = ⟦b⟧` at every hint (every outcome is a proposition, `Data.tyM` at sort 0, so truth agreement is meaning agreement by propext); `evalType` runs the reference from the related state (not the checker state's image) and leaves it untouched (a type is a statement).

**The assemblies** (`Steps/EqJAsm.lean`; now `Eval.eq_step` and `Eval.J_step`; no `sorry` in their bodies). `Eq`: the type, each side on a copy of its own, the checks, `mkEqM`, each a private computation from the state the last left, the extensions composed (`ExtOk.trans`, proved), the outer copy restored, the relation kept, the value (no loan) in flight; the reference's clause (`runT_eq_some`, proved). A side is `side_step`, proved from `eval_step` at the copy's entry and the restore. `J`: the front a private computation; at convertible endpoints the entry and the transported term at the join's grain, which is the reference's clause after its statement positions (`runT_J_some`, proved); at others, the stuck block by `closeBlock_step` (the induction hypothesis), bridged to the reference's `J`; either composed with the front (`SimConclT.of_prefix`, proved). Leaves (16, each a named `sorry` with its paragraph): `Rel.kept`, `inFlight_noLoan`, `copyEntry_ok`, `restore_ext`, `typedAssignment_of_withTemp`, `eq_split`, `eqChecks_kept`, `eq_sides_ok`, `eq_sides`, `eqType_futureFree`, `eq_value_noLoan`, `J_split`, `J_stmts_run`, `J_entry`, `J_arm`, `J_stuck_bridge`. `J_arm` and `J_entry` are the known-constructor arm of a match (entry, arm, join): the match area builds them.

**Concrete** (`Regress/EqJ.lean`): `EqS` accepted, `EqSZ` rejected, `EqFn` (two functions, one generic observation) accepted, `JC` (computed) and `JS` (closed off) accepted, `JWrong` rejected; the reference kernel-checked: `Eq(Nat, S(Z), S(Z))` true and `Eq(Nat, S(Z), Z)` false, the state untouched; `J(Nat, 0, 1, Nat, (), 0)` runs to `0` (the reference's `J` means its transported term whatever the endpoints, [J-run]; the checker is stuck there and closes off, and the block means the run of the `J`).

**Found** (all statement faults, fixed in the open):
1. *A private computation changes the state's core.* `mkEqM_step_stmt` and `evalType_step_stmt` said `st'.core = st.core`: false. A conversion of two functions or Π-types runs their generic calls on a private copy, and `restoreKeep` keeps the new unknowns' types (D37); the table is part of the core. Checked on the checker's state (`Regress/EqJ`): `conv F G`, `mkEqM Nat F G` and the type `Eq(Π(n : Nat). Nat, F, G)` leave Ω's values as they were and one, one and two more unknowns. The conversions said nothing of the state after, which `J` runs its transported term from (incomplete). Now `KeptAfter` and `ExtOk`.
2. *The tail flag of an `evalCore` form is `curTail`.* `eval` reads the one-shot `tailNext`, clears it, and hands it to `evalCore` in `curTail`; the forms read that. D71's fix put `st.tailNext` in `evalCore_step_stmt`, `letIn`, `eq` and `J`, where it is always `false`, so a body's `let` was compared with the reference out of tail position and `eval_step` could not be assembled from `evalCore_step` (found by the `Eq` assembly, whose sides must run out of tail position on both sides). Now `st.curTail`, with `htn : st.tailNext = false` (the clearing) as a hypothesis.
3. *`evalType` ran the reference from the checker state's image* (`SM' K ρ st`), which composes with nothing; now from the related state.

Also: `eq`, `J` and `letIn` lacked `hok` (incomplete: an untyped side, transported term or right-hand side needs it); added. Checked and not found: that a conversion's copy leaves an η record for its generic argument (an unknown of the function's domain, possibly empty, which `TypedAssignment` would then have to type): over an empty one-constructor domain the table grows, no record survives.

**The decisions after a step**: extended with `Δ` (D71's rule paragraph, applied to every extended-form statement at 07c5c3db).

**The data-model lane's rank answer against D73.** Its R1 to R3 are what item 3 asks. R4 is met without a rank fact: `recDecreases_stmt` is stated against the entry parameter's UNKNOWN (its assigned meaning, which no move changes), so the argument at a recursive call is a strict part of the entry as it was passed, and R2 applies. The two things height does not give are already items of the design: the [Rec] link is `recDecreases_stmt`, from a related state, so its assignment is typed and meets the records (`Good`); the predecessor of a number is a lemma about the run's `.matchNat` clause, inside its proof. The run-level lemma (a closure applied to member arguments is its body's run on their meanings) is what item 2's last step uses; two additions: (i) it is needed for a LOCAL closure as well as a global (item 5: the self binding of a `fix` being checked), so it should be stated for any `.clo` meaning, captures included; (ii) with the full closure as the meaning (item 1), it is where the stages appear: the closure at `args` is the stage at `rank args + 1`, and every self call the body makes is below it; nothing else in the design needs the stages. Nothing missing beyond these.

**The final statement** (`substEnv_abs_step_stmt`, "replacing an unknown by what a refinement says it is keeps every meaning", the counterpart for unknowns of the substitution lemma for borrows). From a related, good state, under an assignment at which the refinement `σ := r` holds (`RefineOk`: `ρ σ = ⟦r⟧` at every future and hint, `r` loan-free, naming typed unknowns other than `σ`), the checker's `substEnv (.abs σ) r` over Ω, the stored types, the table and the goal leaves a state with the same shape (`SubstShape`: positions, borrows, declarations; tables only grow) that is related to the SAME reference state with the same renaming (the reference does not split: every position means what it meant) and is good and meets `CtxOk` under an extension (`ExtOk`) at the unknowns the re-runs keep. `substV_abs`, `renormAll`, `refine` and `generalizeNeutral` are stated in the same form; `exitWorld` restores the snapshot, related as it was, with the decisions extended.

**Checked on concrete runs first** (`Regress/SplitSubst.lean`; the checker's states recorded at holes, before a split and inside its last arm; the reference kernel-checked):
1. *A stuck program re-run after the refinement* (where the first relation candidate died, D27). `r = ⌈Pred2(σ0)⌉` before; in the world `σ0 := S(σ1)` the re-run computes `r = σ1`. The reference: under an assignment meeting the world, `⌈Pred2(σ0)⌉`, its text substituted `⌈Pred2(S(σ1))⌉` and `σ1` all mean the same (`refine_keeps_sealed`, with `Pred2` built along its code). A program holding a hole stays sealed with its text substituted (D97: the fills of `PickP(n, &a, &b)` keep `loan_2` and read `PickP(S(σ3), …)`), while the returned borrow's loan-free program re-runs to `σ2`; the re-runs issue labels nothing holds (the counter 4 before, 16 in the world). A stored type rebuilt after the refinement converts two functions (`Eq(Π(n : Nat). Nat, G, Choose(b))` at `b := S(σ3)` is `⊤`), which keeps an unknown: the table grows inside the substitution.
2. *A refinement of an unknown a record or a stored type mentions.* A generalisation record `⌈Pred2(σ0)⌉ := σ1` is not rewritten when `σ0` is refined later; it still names `σ0` and still holds (the same kernel fact). Stored types are rewritten (`y : Box(S(σ2))`, the borrow of it `&Box(S(σ2))`) and mean the same (`refine_keeps_type`, any context); the reference's record of the borrow's content type is a meaning taken when the borrow was made, and agrees.
3. *A refinement under one world, then the join.* After the match, closed off as a block, nothing of either arm remains (path conditions, η and generalisation records empty); the arm's fresh unknown keeps its type and nothing names it; the block's program is what `closeBlock_step` relates. Not kernel-checked: the reference's run of the match selecting the arm (`rfl` does not reduce it; it is `evalMatch`'s clause).

**Assembled, no `sorry` in the bodies** (`Steps/SplitAsm.lean`; now `Subst.refine_step` and `Match.exitWorld_step`): `refine_step_asm` from the substitution (the induction hypothesis), the path condition recorded (`refs_sim`, proved, the slice's) and the re-normalisation (the induction hypothesis), the extensions composed; `exitWorld_step_asm` from its run (`runs_exitWorld_inv`, proved) and `Rel.kept`. Leaves: `meanV_ext`, `exitWorld_ctx`, `access_ctx_ext`.

**Found**:

2. `exitWorld_step_stmt` concluded no `CtxOk`, so no step could follow a world's exit; and with the decisions fixed it is false: the arm's unknowns keep their frozen readings (`absDecl` is the arm's), which `CtxOk`'s alignment asks of every input. Now the decisions are extended and the relation concluded.

### D77. The well-formedness table; the level the checker reads; the substitution lemma for unknowns (fifth lead, 2026-11-20; area (g))

**The table.** Every clause of `Good` (`TypedAssignment`, `Membership`, `WF`) and of `CtxOk`. "Made true by": where what the clause constrains is created (every syntactic clause holds of the empty start state; the typed ones at a definition's generic start, `generic_good`, whose assignment gives each parameter a member). "Writers": the step families that write what the clause reads, each of which must keep it (every step's statement concludes `Good`/`GoodF` and `CtxOk` of its end state, so the obligation sits in each step's statement; the per-family `*_wf_stmt` of `Steps/WF.lean` split some of it out). "Proved kept": where a preservation proof exists. "Recorded": whether `lake exe sweep` checks it on the suite's recorded states (76,568 states).

| Clause | Says | Made true by | Writers (must keep it) | Proved kept | Recorded |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `TypedAssignment.mem` | each named unknown's value is a member of its type | generic start; each step naming an unknown chooses its value (the extension) | parameters, generalisation (`σ := ⟦n⟧`), a split arm's and an η-refinement's fields, private copies' kept unknowns; types rewritten by a refinement (meaning kept, D76) | the record, the `⊥` write, the log (`refs_sim`, `botWrite_good`, `logEffect_rel`) | no (meanings) |
| `TypedAssignment.neutral/eta/refs` | each record is a path condition the assignment meets | `generalizeNeutral`, `etaInPlace`, `refine` (each records what its extension makes true) | `refine` (records not rewritten, still true: D76), `restoreKeep` (a copy's records, the copy good), `exitWorld` (restores) | `refs_sim` | no |
| `Membership.pos` | each position a member of its stored type, at every typed future | generic start | every write of Ω or of a stored type | the `⊥` write only (`botWrite_good`) | no |
| `Membership.absTy/goal` | each named unknown's type and the goal have a level | `freshAbs`, the goal at `checkDef` | `refine`; see (Sort) below | — | no |
| (WF1) | a label has one borrow, a borrow is a whole position | `borrowPlace`, a returned borrow (`closeCall`, D99) | `borrowPlace`, `endBorrow`, [Access], `assignPlace`, a move of a borrow, `popFrame` | — (`borrow/end/assign/close_wf_stmt` stated) | yes |
| (WF2) | outer loans have their borrow, once | same | same | — | yes |
| (WF3) | the loan-under relation is acyclic | same | same | — | yes |
| (WF4) | closures capture no borrow or `⊥`; sealed programs closed | `capture`, `closeCall`, `closeBlock` | same | — (`capture_wf_stmt`, `close_wf_stmt`) | yes |
| (WF6) | names bounded by the counters; table size is the counter | `freshAbs`, `freshLoan` | those, `restoreKeep`, `exitWorld`, `seedHoles` | — (`eval_wf6_stmt`) | yes (+ "never shrinks", D58) |
| (WF7) | a stored `&A`'s content has level 1 | `.ref A`'s D66 check, then `pushBind` | `refine` (types rewritten, levels kept) | — (`eval_wf78_stmt`) | no (meanings) |
| (WF8) | `&` only at the top, never under data or `Eq` | type formation (D48) | `refine`, `pushBind` | — (`eval_wf78_stmt`) | yes |
| (WF9) | a loan outside a program sits at a typed position | `borrowPlace` (a binding lends), `closeCall`'s fills | writes of Ω; values in flight are loan-free after [Access] | — | yes |
| (WF10) | constructor values record their parameters | `evalCtor` (D88, D89) | writes of Ω, untyped re-runs | — (`hintFree_of_wf10` uses it) | yes |
| (WF11) | types and records hold no loan | type formation on private copies; records of loan-free values | `generalizeNeutral` (`n` loan-free), η, `refine` (`r` loan-free) | — (`types_holeFree_stmt` reads it) | yes |
| (WF12) | under a live fill, only borrows filled there | `closeCall`'s fills | `endBorrow` (re-lending), D33 | — | yes |
| (WF14) | no live borrow lent above a moved part | a move | moves, `movedPlace`/`dropAtJoin` (`⊥` writes), D98 | the `⊥` write (`wf14_setAtE`) | yes |
| (WF15) | no recorded reading holds a loan | `capture`, `closeBlock` | same | — | yes |
| (WF16) | no sealed program reads the hint at a tail | `closeCall`, `closeBlock` | substitutions (tails kept) | — | yes |
| `CtxOk.inds/covers/noLabel` | the declarations and globals are the context's | between items (`checkItem`) | none inside a definition (`KeptAfter` carries `inds`) | by the step's form | n/a |
| `CtxOk.delta` | `Δ` is the stored types' code at named unknowns | the start | steps naming unknowns extend `Δ` (D71's rule) | in each extended-form statement | no |
| `CtxOk.globals` | each global means its closure, at its type, a member | `checkDef` | `checkDef` only (the knot, D73) | — | no |
| `CtxOk.dec` | decisions are the checker's | the decisions instance (`DecisionsInst`) | steps that grow the table extend `D` (D71's rule) | `decAligned_grow`, `ShapeFacts.of_sameDec` | no |
| `CtxOk.shapes` (V), (L), (N), (C⋆), (Hη) | a place's content fits its inductive; loans live; constructors of data; `⋆` below a variable at a copy place; η records top-hint-free | writes of constructor values; `borrowPlace`; `evalCtor`; proof fields; `etaInPlace` | writes of Ω, `endBorrow`, D100 (a Π into proofs reads as a copy) | `of_sameDec` (no Ω change) | yes (the slice's (D⋆) moving to a typed clause) |

**Only tested on recorded states** (swept, no preservation proof for any step but the `⊥` write, the log and the records): (WF1)–(WF4), (WF6), (WF8)–(WF12), (WF15), (WF16), (WF14) beyond the `⊥` write, and the five shape facts. **Neither proved nor tested** (they need meanings): `TypedAssignment`, `Membership`, (WF7), `DeltaOk`, `GlobalsOk`, the decisions' alignment.

**The level the checker reads (the candidate).** "A stored type's code has the level `sortOf` computes" (`SortSound`, stated in `Steps/Statements.lean`, with `StoredTy`) is better as a LEMMA than as a clause: `sortOf` reads a type value's head, the declarations (`CtxOk.inds`), an unknown's frozen reading (`absDecl`), a sealed type's head call's declared codomain (the aligned `declOf`) and a Π-type's `piSort` (aligned), so `sortOf_sound_stmt` (stated) derives it from `Good`, `CtxOk` and one small new clause, (Rd) `ReadingsSorted`: an unknown whose frozen reading is `Sort l` has the type `Sort l`. (Rd) is made true by `freshAbsR` (it freezes `valTypeDecl` of the type it pushes, `.sort l` exactly at `Sort l`) and kept by everything (nothing else writes a reading, and `Sort l` holds no unknown, so no substitution changes it). Swept with a one-off probe over the whole suite (76,568 recorded states; the probe is not committed): 40,214 unknowns read as a sort, every one of type `Sort l`; no unknown reads as a proposition (proofs are `⋆`); `sortOf?` reads a sort of every one of the 392,852 stored binding types, so the premise always arises. The model facts it needs (the levels of the codes of `Nat`, `Unit`, `Sort l`, a proposition, `D(ā)` and a telescope, and that a member of `Sort l` is a code of level `l`) are the data-model lane's and `slice1`'s. If the lead prefers a clause: (Sort) itself, established by `pushBind` (the type's sort read at the `let` or parameter), `freshAbs` and the goal's setting, kept by `refine` (heads unchanged, meanings kept), `exitWorld` and `restoreKeep` (restored or kept with their readings).

**The substitution lemma for unknowns** (`Ochr/Meaning/SubstAbs.lean`, beside the core four): `Value.substAbs`/`Term.substAbs` (the pure substitution), `AbsFillOK` (the refinement means `ρ σ` at every hint; the run types the embedded `r` as it types the embedded `σ`; the decisions do not see it, `DecInvariantAbs`), `meanV_substAbs` and `run_substAbs` (open, stated). Which proofs it reuses: for a loan, `meanV_substLoan` is the congruence lemma followed by the same-meaning substitution (`meanV_substSame`/`runT_substSame`, `RunSubst.lean`); for an unknown no congruence step is needed (the assignment already gives `σ` the refinement's meaning), so it IS the same-meaning substitution with the atom `.abs σ` for `.loan ℓ`. That induction reads the atom only in its base case, so the proof is `RunSubst.lean`'s generalised to any atom, not a fifth induction, PROVIDED the base case holds. **It does not, as the run stands** (`absFill_shape_false`, `run_substAbs_false`, kernel-checked): the `.val` clause types an embedded `σ` at `Δ σ` and every other data value at the hint, so `.val σ` and `.val Z` mean the same and are typed `Nat` and `err`, and a `let` stores the difference. On the `SplitSubst` run the meanings and the whole runs agree anyway (`pred2_instance`: a call reads its arguments' meanings only). Two ways out: (1, recommended) the run's `.val` clause types a number at `Nat` and a constructor value that records its parameters at its own type, as the checker does; then `AbsFillOK.shape` holds wherever `DeltaOk` does (a refinement is a value of `σ`'s stored type) and the lemma is the generalised induction (a data clause of `Run.lean`, so after the port); (2) state the lemma up to types, which is a new induction relating states whose binding types differ. The converse substitution (a sealed program replaced by the unknown that means it, `generalizeNeutral`'s) replaces a value, not an atom, and is a separate statement.

|---|---|---|---|---|
| The table (g, D77) | 0 | 0 (one proposed: the `.val` clause's types, after the port) | 0 | 1 (the run-level substitution lemma for unknowns, as the run stands); one candidate clause, (Rd), swept |

### D78. The slice merged; the drop's statements and leaves with the decisions extended; (Rd) a clause (fifth lead, 2026-11-20)

**Merged** `spine-slice` at ed0ee180 (merge f192e4fc, clean): [Access] concludes the typing of places after the access, the helpers after it read the shape facts, (D⋆) `ShapeFacts.dataNotProof` with its sweep mirror, `access_root_not_proof` restated, `copyRead_aligned` and `runs_valTypeDecl_keep` proved, the slice's sweep reports.

**The decisions extended through the drop** (D71's rule; D76's list of the slice's leaves): `Ext` (moved from `MovedPlace` to `DropOneAsm`) gains the decisions `D'` and their agreement with the old ones (`dec : K.D.AgreeOld st.absTy.size D'`), composed by `Ext.trans`. Restated with it: `access_ctx` (the decisions only: [Repack]'s unknown is named by nothing, so the assignment and `Δ` stay; the relation and `Good` the access left carried over), `dropAtJoin_ctx` (likewise: an end inside the drop can repack), `substEnv_abs_sim`, `renormAll_sim`, `placeType_sim`, `freshAbs_sim`, `substEnv_neutral_sim`, `etaFresh_sim`, `placeTypeQ_sim`, `recorded_sim` (now existential: its substitution re-runs programs), `etaPlace_sim`. Re-proved on them, no `sorry` added: `refine_sim'`, `generalizeNeutral_sim`, `etaInPlace_sim`, `mpTail_sim` (the filled-parent branch uses `access_ctx` in its new form), `movedPlace_step`, `dropOne_step_asm`, `joinDrop_step`; the statements `movedPlace_step_stmt`, `dropOne_step_stmt` and `joinDrop_step_stmt` conclude the extended decisions. ### D79. The knot of [Def] built: `step_member_fix` assembled, `Double` through the induction (fifth lead, 2026-11-20)

**The chain no longer stops at the knot.** `step_member_fix` (`Consistency.lean`) is assembled with no `sorry` in its body: the function branch of `checkDef` taken apart (`fix_split`, a leaf), the induction on the bound (`Steps.stages_total`, proved: if from the guarded fixpoint good below `b` the stage `b + 1` is good at member arguments of rank `b`, the fixpoint is good at every member argument), and the closure lemma's membership half (`Steps.closure_mem_of_total`, the data layer's). The chain's open leaves are now `const_split`, `typedFutureF_exists`, `const_value`, `fix_split`, `accepted_resolves`, `covered_prefix`, `false_means_empty`, `falseConst_ty`, `exists_dataModel`, `resolves_of_accepted`, `resolvesTo_unique`.

**What changed** (D73's design): `RunCtx.cur : Option (String × Nat)` (default `none`; a field of the structure, no data clause); `GlobalsOk.mem` asks membership of every global but the one `cur` names, and `GlobalsOk.memBelow` asks of that one only `MemBelow b` (applied by `callOf` at member arguments whose decreasing component has `semRank` below `b`, defined and a member of the codomain; `ArgsMember`, `decRank`); `GlobalsOk.of_globals` (proved) carries it across states with the same globals. `Regress/DefKnot.ctx_contains_fix_member` now needs `cur = none`, and `memBelow_zero` (proved) shows the knot cut: at the bound `0` nothing of the conclusion is assumed.

**Named statements the knot takes** (`Steps/FixKnot.lean`): owed by the data layer, `closure_mem_of_total` and `closure_apply` (the two halves of the run-level closure lemma, at any closure meaning, captures included) and `semRank_part` (R2, with `SemPart`: a field of a constructor value or a number's predecessor, transitively; `semRank` is the height after the port; R1 holds since `semRank` is a total function of the meaning, R3 since there is no separate element after the port); the spine's, `recDecreases` ([Rec]'s test, a strict subterm of the parameter's entry as the path conditions expand it, means a part of the entry's meaning under a good assignment) and `fix_split`, whose second half is the induction's step: the checker's run of the body read through the step statements under the context naming the definition at the bound, with its global meaning the stage-`b` closure, so that the checker's `self` and the reference's agree (`MemBelow b` of it from the hypothesis through `closure_apply` and stage stability). That needs `checkTail_step_stmt` and `generic_good_stmt` migrated to the simulation form first (they are in the old form).

**Concrete, kernel-checked** (`Regress/DefKnot.lean`): the checker accepts `Double (n : Nat) : Nat by n := match n { Z => Z, S m => S(S(Double(m))) }` and rejects `Same (n : Nat) : Nat by n := Same(n)` ([Rec]); `Double`'s closure as the `.fix` clause builds it (its body as the function on meanings it computes, `CodeClosures.dblBody`, its stages with filled closures) goes through the induction: the step holds at every bound (`double_step`, the stage `b + 1` doubles `b`), so the fixpoint is good at every member argument (`double_total`, by `stages_total`), and its closure is a member of its type (`double_closure_member`: the closure lemma's membership half on this instance). Not kernel-checked: the reference's run of `Double`'s own `fix` term (its body's run is written as the function on meanings, as in `CodeClosures`).

**The one place the context's meaning of a global differs from `defMeaning`'s**: inside the induction's step at the bound `b` (the second half of `fix_split`), the definition's global means the stage-`b` closure, so that the checker's `self` (`.gfn f`) and the reference frame's `self` mean the same at the generic state; everywhere else, the chain's context gives a global the meaning `defMeaning` gives it (the full closure).

**Gaps named**: local closures (D73 item 5: the self binding of a `fix` formed in a body) take the same form through `Membership` and are not done. (The borrow parameters' final contents: done in D80.)

### D81. Every value the machine holds has a stored type: typed temporaries (sixth lead, 2026-11-20; designed, prototype stated, not built)

**Found** (`Regress/EvalMemGap.lean`, kernel-checked, standard axioms): no statement concluded that an evaluation returns a member of its type, because a temporary has no stored type and `Membership` speaks only of typed positions. Three faults with that root: `bodyReturns_sound_stmt` and `repackCheck_sound_stmt` are FALSE ([Repack] returns at once on a value with no dependent constructor, so `0` passes at `Unit`); a typed `let` cannot be assembled (`Good` after its push contains the value's membership, `good_withBind_mem`, which `eval_step`'s conclusion does not give, `not_good_push`); and a borrow held by a temporary leaves its label free in every typed future, so its typed lender is no member and `Good` is false (`borrowArg_not_good`): the call statements' hypothesis `Good (st.withTemps (fv :: ws))` is unsatisfiable at a borrow argument, and so is `Good` at the pop, a `let`'s drop or the join's drop whenever the value in flight is a borrow of a typed place.

**Decided (proposed to the orchestrator): a temporary has a stored type, as a binding has.** Each push stores the type the machine has in hand; the existing clauses then cover temporaries: `Membership.pos` is the evaluation's membership and every later step keeps it as it keeps a binding's (a call's earlier arguments, a constructor's fields, a result across a `let`'s drop, the join, a frame's pop), and `TypedFuture` types the label of a borrow held by a typed temporary (the missing record: the content type `evalCore`'s `.borrow` returns). The alternative, a conjunct "the returned value is a member" in `SimConclT`, says nothing once the next step runs; every step that can run with a value in flight below it would need a conjunct keeping that value's membership, which is this clause assembled piece by piece, and labels stay untyped. Prototype (`Steps/EvalMem.lean`): the clause with the temporaries' types abstracted (`TempTys`, `GoodT`), the projection to the value in flight (`goodT_push_mem`, proved), the conclusion form (`SimConclTT`, `eval_step_stmtT`), a new clause `MembershipT.tys` (stored types are types: conversion's premise). Checked (`Regress/EvalMemRuns.lean`): the state with `&x` in flight, good at no assignment today, is good at every assignment with the temporary typed `&Nat` (`goodT_SB`); the value a typed `let`, a match on a known number, a call of a known closure and a constructor (`Cons(3, Cons(4, Nil))`, at the probes' data model) return is a member of the type returned.

**Where the type lives**: recommended, a field of the checker's frame beside its temporaries, written at the 18 push sites (each has the type in hand, at the site or one call up: `assignPlace`, `dropValue`, `obsResult`, `endBorrowArgs` take it as a parameter); no verdict changes. The model-side form (`Good` taking the types as a parameter) threads through 28 files.

**The checker change, for the checker lane** (decided by the orchestrator 2026-11-20: the type lives in the checker's frame). `Frame` gains, beside `temps : Array Value`, the stored type of each temporary (`Option Value`; e.g. `tempTys : Array (Option Value)` kept the same length, or `temps : Array (Value × Option Value)`). Each push stores the type the machine already holds for the value at that moment, never one computed for the purpose; `none` where it holds none (an untyped run's result whose type was not demanded, D82). No verdict changes. Line numbers are `Ochr/Machine.lean` at spine b2992440 (= spine's copy of main's checker; find each by its function on `main`).

Beside the pushes: `popTemp`/`popTemps` (`Env.lean` 512, 518) pop the type with the value; `setAt (.temp …)` (`Env.lean` 536) keeps the type; `tyAt (.temp …)` (`Env.lean` 540, today `none`) returns it; `mapState` with `types` (`Env.lean` 565) rewrites a temporary's stored type as it rewrites a binding's (a refinement reaches it). Readers of values only (`allPos`, `valAt`, `Stability.lean` 133, `Located.lean` 61, `obsFrame`'s `temps.size`, `endLoansOfTopTemp` 1257) are unchanged. In an untyped run (`typed = false`), sites 6–9 and 12–14 store whatever the untyped `eval` returned, `some` only where D82 demanded the type (borrow arguments are demanded: `evalCall`'s `demand (ad matches .ref _)`).

**Untyped runs** (instance runs of known callees, re-runs): the value in flight owes nothing (its temporary untyped when no type is in hand). But the invariant ALREADY owes, inside an untyped run, membership at positions bound at a type the run read and never checked (D82's demanded types; `Regress/EvalMemRuns`, part 3: the untyped `let x = (0 : Unit); x` runs and binds `x : Unit ↦ 0`), and η at `Unit` there (parameters, call results) needs it. True on accepted code (the generic run checked the same body) but established by no step of the untyped run. Open; two routes: (A) positions written by an untyped run owe no membership and the reference makes the membership-dependent decisions alike (η at `Unit` in `callFrame` and `callOf`), so the simulation needs none there; (B) a theorem that an instance run of a generically checked body keeps every position a member (docs/35 Theme 2). Leaning (A), after a census of the membership-dependent decisions an untyped run makes. A borrow in flight in an untyped run with no type in hand keeps `Flight` (the reference's type); whether that case occurs is to be measured.

**The return path**: membership at the declared type comes from the evaluation's membership at the type it returned, conversion's soundness at the two types (`Rules/ComposeT.conv_sound_T` on the trial, under "well typed inside"), wholeness, and [Repack]'s honest content (`RepackSound`, a guess); composed in `return_member` (proved). `step_member_const` and the knot's `fix_split` rest on the false [Return] statement and are to be re-assembled this way (`const_split` also omits the conversion the checker runs).

**Strict meanings**: the clause is membership (`pmem`). With strict meanings a type value's membership in a universe is "well typed inside" (already partly so: `Type₀`'s carrier excludes the error code), and conversion's premises follow from it; without them the clause must be the trial's `HWT` at every position.

### D82. The inventory of open statements (sixth lead, 2026-11-20; `Ochr/Meaning/OPEN.md`, kept current)

Every `sorry`'d named statement, by area, with what it says, whether it was checked, a size guess, a truth guess and whether it is about the machine alone; read by four read-only agents, spot-checked by the lead. About 165 open statements: 38 machine-only (the `grows_*` family, `DecTab.back`, `access_absTy`, the resolution facts), most of the rest in the simulation form. Two found false and kernel-checked on the way (`Regress/OpenFalse.lean`): `grows_restoreKeep` (needs the snapshot's globals and declarations to be the current ones) and `typedFutureF_exists` (needs the flight's types inhabited). Three root causes recur in the suspect rows: untyped temporaries (D81), untyped runs (D81), and the comparisons' leak (docs/35; D103 on main). The five most likely false, not yet kernel-checked: the decisions instance cannot meet `DecTab` (one fuel against every fuel); the evaluation statements at a typed hole of an empty type (no `NoHoles` hypothesis; `consistency_rel` never uses it); `assignPlace_step` (no membership of the written value); `runBody_front` (parameters bound unchecked); and three call-tail leaves with unconstrained variables.

### D83. The strict reference run: the census, and the specification `model1` builds from (sixth lead, 2026-11-20; decided (C) with (C1), see D86; to be built on `spine-model`)

**Why.** An untyped run (a known callee's body run in place by `Machine.runBody`; a sealed program's re-run by `Machine.nfSealed`) writes positions at types it never checks (`Regress/EvalMemRuns` part 3: the untyped `let x = (0 : Unit); x` binds `x : Unit ↦ 0`), so `Good`'s membership there cannot come from a check. Every untyped step is stated under `hok`, "the reference run of this term succeeds", supplied from above (a callee's body: the callee's meaning is a member of its function type, so its run on member arguments succeeds; a re-run: the sealed program's meaning is a member where it sits, so its run succeeded; sub-runs of a successful run succeed). If the reference run is STRICT, failing wherever a value is not a member of the type it is given, then `hok` carries the membership, and the simulation (the checker's value means the reference's) puts it at the position. The reference is not strict today: `Regress/OpenFalse.run_ascribe_trusted` (kernel-checked) runs `(0 : Unit)` to `0` typed `Unit`. With a strict ascription, `let x = (0 : Unit); x` has no successful reference run, `hok` is false, and the untyped step owes nothing there.

**Notation.** "Strict at `T`" means: the clause returns its result only if the value is a member of `T` in the model, and fails (`none`) otherwise; the test is classical (the run is noncomputable). "Member" is `v ∈ₛ[K.dm.read] T` for a whole value and `DataModel.pmem K.dm.read K.Γ v T` (possibly partly moved, `Data.lean`) where the value can have moved parts; each row says which. File and line references are `Ochr/Meaning/Run.lean` at spine 8a966499.

**The census**: every point where a checker run with `typed = false` writes a position that has a type, or makes a decision that depends on membership (`Machine.lean` sites given).

| # | Checker point | Reference clause today (`Run.lean`) | Strict: what it must check | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | a `let`'s binding at the demanded type (`evalCore` `.letIn`, `pushBind h T v`, D82's `demand true`) | `.letIn` (749): binds at the right-hand side's run type, no check | nothing of its own: once every clause producing a typed result is strict (rows 3–8, 12), the binding holds a member of its run type, which is the checker's by `StrAgree` | covered by rows 3–8, 12 |
| 2 | a parameter bound at its declared type, η at `Unit` (`runBodyCore`, `pushBind h A (etaUnit pd w)`) | `callOf` (508) through `callOfL` (471): decodes each argument at its parameter's code, fails on a non-member | already strict | covered: `ApplyMem.app_mem'`, `app_mem_unit` (PROVED) |
| 3 | an assignment `p := u` (`evalCore` `.assign` then `assignPlace`; untyped: no `expectTy`) | `.assign` (739) then `assignAt` (399): writes, no check | `assignAt` strict at the place's content type (the `T` that `.assign` already reads from `contentOf` as the right-hand side's hint), `pmem` (a value with moved parts can be written back) | to build |

| 9 | a call's result at the declared codomain, η at `Unit` (`callFn`), [Close]'s result and fills (`closeCall`) | `callOf`: result at the codomain code | already | covered: `app_mem'`, `app_mem_unit`, `app_mem_finals`, `callOfL_mem` (PROVED) |

| 12 | a proof (any erased term: a proof call, a `Prop` constructor, `cong`, `symm`, `trans`, `rewrite`, `J` at a proposition, a proof-valued `let`/`seq`/match, an erased place) bound at its proposition | `SM.erasedIf` (89): `⋆` UNRUN, typed `err`; `SM.erasedAt` (113): `⋆` at the place's stored type, unrun | (C1), below | to build |
| 13 | generalising a stuck program, η-refining an unknown (`evalMatch`, `etaInPlace`) | the assignment gives the new unknown the program's meaning | not needed: a member by the position it came from (`Good`, kept by rows 1–12) | covered |

**(C1): erased terms run, strictly, at their propositions.** Today the reference never runs a proof, so its success certifies no proposition, in typed runs as much as untyped ones (`proof_step` concludes the proposition true and no statement covers the checker's typed run of the proof term on its copy: `evalAt` runs it with `evalCore`, and `evalCore_step` requires the term not erased). The change: `SM.erasedIf fl x` with `fl.2` (a proof) runs `x` on a copy (`SM.onCopy`), discards the state, and returns `(⋆, T)` where `T` is the type `x` returned, only if that run succeeds and its result is a member of `T` (for a proposition: `T` is true). So every clause that can produce a proof must return the proposition it proves as its type:
- a proof call: the callee's codomain at the arguments (`callOf`, already); a constructor of a `Prop`-sorted declaration: `dm.tyR d r` (`ctorOut`, already; true by `proof_mem_tyR` given row 5);
- `refl` is the library's `I : True` (`Prelude.lean`), a constructor: covered by the line above;
- `cong f h` (`Machine.evalCore` `.cong`): from `h`'s run at `Eq(A, a, b)` (meaning `prop (a = b)`), the proposition `f a = f b` at the codomain of `f` (both by `callOf`); at `True`, `True`;
- `symm h`: from `Eq(A, a, b)`, `b = a`; `True` to `True`; `False` to `False`;
- `trans h k`: from `Eq(A, a, b)` and `Eq(A, b', c)` with `b = b'`, `a = c`; `True` with `T` gives `T`; `False` with anything gives `False`;
- `rewrite h u`, `rewriteR h u`: the goal `G` (the clause's hint), given `u`'s run at the rewritten goal and `h`'s at `Eq(A, a, b)`;
- `J A a b P h u` at a proposition: `P(b)`, given `u` at `P(a)` and `h` at `Eq(A, a, b)`;
- a proof-valued `let`, `seq` or match: its tail's or arm's proposition, as today for data;
- an erased place (`SM.erasedAt`): `⋆` at the place's stored type, a member by the state's invariant (the place holds a member), no run needed.
Today these primitives return `(⋆, .plain err)` (999). Cost: the erased clauses, a type per proof primitive, the four core lemmas re-proved (`meanV_substLoan`, `run_substLoan`, `meanV_congr`, `run_congr`), and the regress runs that go through erased terms.

**The two points (C) does not cover.** Closures formed in a run (row 11): the local knot, D73 item 5, not designed; typed runs need it equally. Holes (row 15): excluded by hypothesis.

**A technical point for the derivation.** `Good` asks membership under every typed future at ONE extended assignment `ρ'`; the simulation is stated per future `φ` (the induction hypothesis is at every `φ`, each with its own `∃ ρ'`). Taking the membership at every future from the reference needs the extension's values not to depend on the future, as `Steps/CallAsm.nested_uniform` asks of re-runs (a loan-free run reads the future only at labels it makes).

**Found on the way.** Suspect, by reading the two clauses, not yet kernel-checked: at a proof-valued `let`, the reference binds at `err` (the erased right-hand side's type) and the checker at the proposition, so the skeletons differ (`Rel.skel` compares the binding's code) and the tail has no related state; (C1) removes it, since the erased run then returns the proposition.

### D84. The chain's decision table is read with no fuel; holes; `typedFutureF_exists` restated (sixth lead, 2026-11-20; built)

**Found, kernel-checked** (`Regress/OpenFalse.decTab_fixedFuel_false`): the chain's instance `checkerDecisions st fuel` reads each decision at ONE fuel and answers a default where it runs out, while `DecTab` asks agreement with the checker at EVERY fuel at which the decision succeeds. At fuel `1` the reading of `&x` (with `x` read `Unit`) is the default `other`, at fuel `3` it is `&Unit`; at any fuel a term nested deeper does the same. So `CtxOk` was unsatisfiable at the chain's decisions and every step statement vacuous there.

**Fixed** (`Meaning/DecisionsFF.lean`, the orchestrator's choice): the table's answer is the checker's answer at any fuel at which the decision succeeds, a default where it never does (`readDecisionFF`, a classical choice; `checkerDecisionsFF`). It meets `DecTab` (`decTab_ff`, PROVED) given one fact about the machine alone, stated for the machine-only lane: successful runs of a decision at different fuels agree (`decide_fuel_agree`, over the eight decisions `DecTab` reads; the decision procedures catch nothing). The chain instantiates `chainDecisions fuel` (fuel-free but for D90's [Entry] captures, whose clause is leaving `CtxOk` for the [Entry] step's hypothesis); the chain's leaves and assemblies (`const_split`, `fix_split`, `fix_sortRule`, `fix_canon`, `step_member_*`, `defn_member`, `consistency_rel`) take `hDec : ∀ st, DecAligned (D st) st`, discharged at the top by `chainDecisions_aligned` (assembled from `decTab_ff` and the captures' leaf `chainDecisions_blockCaps`). No step statement changed. Concrete runs that read a decision's value (`Regress/EntryCheck`, `Regress/JoinProbes`, `#guard`s on the fixed-fuel instance) still check the checker's decisions; they reach the chain's table through `readDecisionFF_of_runs` (a successful run at some fuel fixes the answer). `DecideScope.checkerDecisions_*` (unused) are about the fixed-fuel instance.

**Holes** (the orchestrator's decision): the evaluation statements take "the term holds no hole", threaded from `consistency_rel`'s `NoHoles`, which it never used. The reason: `eval_step_stmt` is false at a typed hole (`OpenFalse.eval_hole_false`), and `Ochr/Consistency.holeFalse` is an accepted proof of `False` by a hole. Form (to build with the next statement change): `t.holeFree` on `eval_step_stmt`, `evalCore_step_stmt` and the per-form statements, and a clause that no code in the state (closures, sealed programs, globals) holds a hole, for bodies and re-runs.

**`typedFutureF_exists` restated and PROVED**: a typed future exists where each label's constraints (its typed holders' types and its flight entries) have a common member (`LabelsInhabited`); at its use the members are the reference's records (`labelsInhabited_of_rel`, a leaf; restated with typed temporaries, where the flight goes).

### D85. An accepted proof of `False`: a nested declaration that uses its own parameter negatively (recorded for the orchestrator, 2026-11-20)

Found by the agent proving that a data model exists (`exists_dataModel`, the chain's one leaf nobody had attacked), reproduced by the orchestrator: `inductive Q (A : Type) (b : Bit) := MkQ(y : FB(A, b), z : OptB(Q(Q(A, b), B1), b))` is accepted, where `FB(A, b)` is a function type from `A` only at one value of `b`. `Q` is nested at its own parameter, which it uses negatively through a type function; the checker skipped the nesting condition for a declaration's own occurrences. So `Ochr.Consistency.consistency` is false of the checker as it stands (`origin/spine-exists`, `Meaning/DataModel/Exists/ConsistencyCE.lean`). Fixed in the checker as main's D107 (see D92 and D102). The same agent found `exists_dataModel` false three further ways at any model (a junk type parameter, the parameter list's length, the frame the field types run in), all being repaired in the data layer, which that agent owns.

### D86. The plan of record: a fundamental lemma of the model, and the simulation (the orchestrator's decision on D83, 2026-11-20)

**Decided: (C) with (C1).** The reference run is made strict (D83's rows 3–8) and runs erased terms strictly at their propositions (D83's (C1)). The proof then has two halves that meet only at "the strict reference run succeeds":
- **(I) The fundamental lemma of the model** (no checker in it): from a model state whose positions hold members of their types, a successful strict run returns a member of the type it returns and leaves such a state; for an erased term, success means its proposition is true. One induction over the run's clauses, using `ApplyMem.app_mem'` (calls), `CtorMem.ctorOut_mem` (constructors), `ReadMember.field_mem` and `follow_below_member` (reads). It is part (b) of the main theorem, and the analogue of the soundness theorem of Lean's metatheory (a well-typed term's interpretation is an element of its type's). Built by `model1` (branch `spine-model`), prototype first on three clauses and one proof primitive.
- **(II) The simulation**: the checker's typed run succeeds, so the strict reference run succeeds and is related to it. The checker's checks serve this half only: a successful check makes the strict clause succeed (conversion's soundness at an ascription, an assignment, a field, a return, an argument). Untyped runs get `hok` from the callee's membership and the frame property, as now.
- Typed temporaries (D81) stay: they are where `Good` says a value in flight is a member. The table of who owes what (`OPEN.md`) regroups into the cases of (I) and the checks of (II).
docs/18 is brought up to date once `model1`'s prototype confirms the shape. (Done: docs/18 was rewritten on 2026-11-21, main 63110a28. Since this entry: the read lemmas named in (I), `field_mem` and `follow_below_member`, were for the read by name and are deleted, replaced by `follow_good`, D99; and (I) is not yet proved in a form that says anything, D95 and D98.)

**Recorded from the machine-only lane** (branch `spine-machine`, 35614694): "the tables only grow" is proved for the whole machine by one simultaneous induction, except nine steps held for checker changes (so the induction is not closed: `machineGrows` carries `sorryAx`; D88). Two stated facts were false and have corrected forms in `Steps/MachineMono.lean`: `grows_restoreKeep` (`restoreKeep_grows` assumes the snapshot has the current globals and declarations; `Regress/OpenFalse.grows_restoreKeep_false` is the counterexample) and `grows_closeBlock` (`grows_closeBlock_of` assumes every arm only extends the tables: an arm's world exit keeps its frozen readings and counters). `Steps/EqJAsm.lean` is to use the corrected forms. That lane also found that the checker's block-captures granularity loop is a `while`, which Lean builds on a partial function, so nothing about `blockCaptures`, `closeBlock` or what reaches them can be proved until it is bounded by the fuel: a violation of the checker's totality standard, being fixed in the checker.

### Entries D87 to D108 (the orchestrator, 2026-11-21): what was built and found from D86 to `spine` 2afd418c (D87 to D103), D104 from the model lane's working branch later that morning, and D105 to D108 at `spine` 2a7982f7

Written in one pass from the lanes' commit messages and working notes (the `HANDOFF.md` files beside the code, which are deleted when a lane's work is merged; what was durable in them is here). The checker's own decisions are cited as "main's D-number", since the two numberings overlap. Statuses were checked at 2afd418c, or on the branch named, by `#print axioms`.

### D87. The decision table is fixed for the whole check of an item (main's decision, 2026-11-20; built by the slice lane, 1f328465)

**Replaces** D57's and D71's rule that a step which creates unknowns concludes an extended decision table `D'` agreeing with the old one, and D78's extension of it through the drop.

**What.** The context's decisions `K.D` are those of the item's final state and never change. Every extended-form statement (46 in `Steps/Statements.lean`) takes `(hD : DecTab K.D st')`, the decisions aligned with the checker's tables at the state after the step, in place of concluding `∃ D', K.D.AgreeOld … D' ∧ …`; the assignment `ρ'` and `Δ'` still extend. `DecAligned` is `DecTab` (the decisions that read only the tables, which transfer backwards along a run) extended by the block's captures (which read values, per state). Deleted: `D'` everywhere, `Decisions.AgreeOld` and its lemmas, `Steps/DecExtend.lean` (`meanV_congrD`, `runT_congrD`, `decAligned_grow`), and the `D'` component of `Ext`, `ExtOk`, `SimConcl`, `SimConclT`, `FillRel`.

**Why.** With an extended table each step needed a transfer lemma for every meaning it had established, and D57 had already found two cases the transfer did not cover (the unknowns grow at ends of borrows; a refinement rewrites the types of existing unknowns). Nothing reads a decision at an unknown not yet created, so one table for the run is enough, and what is owed instead is a fact about the checker alone (D88): the rest of a run only extends the tables, and alignment at a larger table gives alignment at a smaller one (`DecTab.back`, `DecTab.of_rest`). D84 then made the table itself fuel-free.

**Cost found on the way.** Every application of the induction hypothesis needs the growth of the REST of the run after it, so the facts are per machine function and a closure tactic (`grows_go`) proves the rests. The tactic must match by reducible unfolding only, or `whnf` of a large rest (`movedPlace`'s tail) times out at any budget.

### D88. The facts about the checker alone have their own file and lane (2026-11-20; `Steps/MachineMono.lean` on `spine`, `Steps/Machine/` on `spine-machine` 7f1f1c9a, not merged)

**What.** `TablesGrow st st'` (globals and declarations kept, the frozen readings of old unknowns kept, counters not lowered) and `GrowsM x` (every successful run of `x` only extends the tables), with their closure lemmas (bind, pure, get, a table-keeping `modify`, `if`, loops), proved on `spine`. One `grows_<function>` per machine function an assembly's rest runs, and `DecTab.back`: on `spine` all of these are `sorry`.

**On the lane's branch.** The property is restated relative to a table, `GrowsIn tb x`, because a private copy's exit takes the globals and declarations from the saved state, which keeps them only if the saved state had them. One simultaneous induction over the machine's 113 mutually recursive functions (`MachineGrows tb nf`, one field per function; `Zero.lean`, `Step*.lean`, `All.lean`), with a tactic that walks a goal by the head constant of the computation and never unfolds. **It is not closed:** `machineGrows` carries `sorryAx` through nine steps held in `StepPending.lean`: the comparison functions (`conv`, `convList`, `convTList`, `convPi`, `convT`, `convFn`, `convFnRun`) and `dropAtJoin`, held for checker changes, and `blockCaptures`, whose granularity loop was a `while`. So "the tables only grow, for the whole machine" (D86's wording) is true of each function given the ones it calls, not yet of the machine. The changes the nine waited for are on main now (main's D102, D103, D106 and D109; see entry D102 below).

**Decisions agree across fuels** (the fact D84's `decTab_ff` assumes, `decide_fuel_agree`): proved on the lane for each decision `DecTab` reads (`FuelLe`, `decideFuelLe`, `fuelAgree_flags` and its siblings): a decision procedure never touches the budget or the log, and each function of Decide's block at `n` is the same at `n + 1` wherever it succeeds. Not for the block's captures, which run the machine.

**Two statements were false as written.** `grows_restoreKeep` (`Regress/OpenFalse.grows_restoreKeep_false`, and `Machine/False.not_grows_restoreKeep` on the lane): a restore from a snapshot with other globals does not keep them; the corrected `restoreKeep_grows` assumes `TablesGrow saved cur`, and `Steps/EqJAsm.eq_step_asm` goes through with it (checked on a copy). `grows_closeBlock` with no hypothesis on the arms: an arm that lowers the frozen readings leaves them lowered, since the world's exit keeps the arm's; the corrected `grows_closeBlock_of` assumes every arm only extends the tables. No kernel counterexample for the second: every run of `closeBlock` went through the `while`.

**A finding for the checker.** `while` is `Loop.forIn`, implemented by a `partial def`: the kernel cannot see into it, so nothing about `blockCaptures`, `closeBlock` or what reaches them could be proved. Main's D109 bounds the loop by the fuel.

**Why a lane of its own.** These proofs have no model in scope, and they were the cheapest and most reliable work of the week; D101 found the same of the machine's half of conversion. The file's docstring lists the other facts of this kind the proof owes: an access loses no unknown the state names (swept, D58); a decision's run leaves the state as it was; fresh names are never reused within an item.

### D89. A recursive function's stages are closures at a weakened function type; the fill is deleted (data layer, 2026-11-20; built)

**Replaces** D60's filled stages, and the two closure statements `Steps/FixKnot.lean` first carried.

**Found** (`ClosureCE.lean`, kernel-checked, for every data reading): `closure_mem_of_total` and `closure_apply` as first stated were false four ways. A closure is built strictly, so a function defined at one argument only makes the closure `err`, and the call fails. At sort `0` the closure is `⋆` and its type the proposition that the codomain is true at every argument. At a returned borrow the carrier holds only functions with an injective backward function and a returned label, which `OutGood` did not ask. And a function of sort `Type₀` returning types has no code at level 0 to decode its result at.

**What.** Stage `k + 1` of a `fix` binds `self` to stage `k`'s closure typed at the WEAKENED function type: the true codomain where the decreasing argument's rank is below `k`, and `Unit` (at a data sort) or `True` (at sort 0) above it (`weakCod l rk b out`, `stageFn`, `fillTy`/`fillVal`, in `Apply1.lean` so that `Run.lean` can use them). So every stage is a member of a type, with nothing chosen classically: `stage_mem`, `stage_apply` (below the bound, the result at the TRUE codomain), `stage_mem_prop`, `stage_mem_ref`, `stage_apply_ref` (`ClosureStage.lean`, `ClosureRef.lean`, with `RetGood` for a returned borrow). The run's own `.fix` clause builds its stages this way (the lead's decision: the uniform endpoint, not a relation to the stages afterwards), mirrored in `RunCongr.fixStage`; the function's meaning is still stage `rank + 1`, and the final closure is taken at the true type. Deleted: `mkTelFillL`, `closureFillL`, `closureFillAt`, `LeafDefL` and their lemmas.

**The knot on it** (`Steps/Statements.lean`, `Steps/FixKnot.lean`): `MemBelow` restated (the definition's global is the stage closure at the weakened type, of a function good below the bound by `OutGood'`); `PiFits` (what membership consumes) and `PiCallable` (what a call consumes besides); `GlobalsOk.ty` and `.fn` are not asked of the definition being checked. Proved: `memBelow_mem`, `memBelow_zero`, `memBelow_apply`, `closure_mem_of_total` in its true form, `stages_below` (the induction in the run's shape, replacing `stages_total`), `knot_member`; `step_member_fix` is assembled through `knot_member`. `fix_split` is restated on `OutGood'` and is the knot's one reader of the return check.

**Open leaves:** `fix_sortRule` (the sort rule of the definition's function type as the run reads its parts; stronger than `sortOf_sound_stmt` as stated), `fix_canon` (the run's type values are canonical codes: an induction like `RunPresInd`, not small), `recDecreases`. `piFits_of_rules` turns the first two into `PiFits` and `PiCallable`. The knot for a `fix` formed inside a run (D73 item 5) is still not designed.

### D90. Three facts about the model, proved (data layer, 2026-11-20; `RunSubstAbs.lean`, `ApplyMem.lean`, `DataModel/CtorMem.lean`)

- **The substitution lemma for unknowns** (`meanV_substAbs`, `run_substAbs`; D76 stated it): under an assignment that gives an unknown the meaning of a value, substituting the value for the unknown changes no meaning and no run. The file is `RunSubst.lean`'s induction with the atom renamed; only the two base cases are new. A shortcut, flagged: the two files duplicate one induction of about 550 lines, and the uniform form is one substitution generic in the atom.
- **Application lands in the result type** (`app_mem'`, `app_mem`, `app_mem_ref`, `app_mem_finals`, `callOfL_mem`, `app_mem_unit`): a member of a function type is an element of its code's carrier whatever built it, so applying it where the arguments reach the codomain (each decodes at its parameter's code) gives a member of the codomain, for results, final contents and a returned borrow, with η at `Unit`. Needs that an encoded element is a member of its code's type, proved at the three levels.
- **A constructor value from member fields is a member** (`ctorR_mem`, `proof_mem_tyR`, `ctorOut_mem`, with `mem_fitsOneM`, the converse of `fitsOneM_mem`): at the record the run's clause knows, whatever gave it. The check on the parameter-solving probe found its hand signature out of date.

These are the three facts (I) uses at a call, a constructor and a split (D95).

### D91. Records keep a value parameter read small; a parameter is solved back at any depth (data layer, 2026-11-20; with main's D105)

**What.** `Entry.small (s : Small)`: a record keeps a value parameter of a level-0 type as its small reading at the parameter's key, so the run's constructor clause can read it back off a field's type: `MkW(t)` with `t : Tag(3)` is given `Wrap(3)` (`ParamSolve.sw_ctor`, `so_ctor`). `unifyTyS`/`unifyArgsTyS` read a type parameter back through the records' entries at any depth (e1af9662), and an entry `.small sv` against a variable fills it with `ofSmall sv`. `RecordsFaithful` became a structure: `types`, `values` (a value parameter that is a member is kept small at its key), `smallOnly` (parameter lists that differ only at such parameters with equal small readings give one record).

**A guess under it:** `smallOnly` for the canonical model rests on "a member is determined by its small reading and its type", argued by induction on membership and not proved. The least sure part: a type function whose result embeds the parameter through some channel other than an equation or a data type's argument.

**What the model cannot follow, and the checker's answer.** A parameter that stands in the fields' types only at positions of a higher universe is kept by no record (`ParamSolve.sh_fails`). Main's D105: such a parameter is not inferred; it must be written or come from the expected type. This closes docs/18's one known disagreement between the model's and the checker's parameter inference.

### D92. Records are functions of paths over one signature; the existence statement restated, and its proof decomposed (existence lane, 2026-11-20; ported at 4c749d49 and 928da269)

**Found.** `exists_dataModel` as stated was false for EVERY model at `Box (A : Type) := MkBox(x : A)`, three ways: a junk type parameter, a parameter list too short, and the readings of the frame the field types run in (kernel-checked at 8b5f60f2 in `StatementCE.lean`, since removed because it targets the old definitions). And the layout made existence circular: a type's record held the records of the types nested in it, which had to be built by the runs the model gives meaning to.

**What.** A record never holds a record: `Entry` is `none`, `leaf B c`, `data d` or `small s`, and the record of a nested type is the record's continuation past the key (`DataLib/Universal.lean`: `kindKey`, `contStep`, `paramKey`, `paramContStep`, `contRec`). The table of field shapes is then one function with no program in it (`uShape`, `uSig`), reading of the earlier fields only the index fields (`projIdx`, `uShape_idx`: `FieldTyIdx` at every record, by construction). `DataModel := {covers, recOf, paramTy}`; `dm.sig` is a definition. A leaf's type is its code (so junk codes are ordinary leaves); `RecLeafOk` asks that no leaf hold a data code over the model's own carrier. Faithfulness (`DataModel/Faithful.lean`) is conditional on the run being defined, fixes the parameter list's length, and runs field types in `teleFrame` (fields at the checker's verdict of proof or not, parameters at their declared readings). `Run.lean` changed in one line. The hand models are records of paths.

**The limit construction** (`DataModel/Canonical/Layout.lean`, `Knot.lean`): a declaration's own record as the limit of approximations by path length (`KnotSpec`, `approx`, `recK`; `recK_kind`, `recK_cont`, `recK_agree`, `recK_shape`: faithful at a field), which exists when the declaration's own occurrences are guarded. A `Prop`-sorted declaration by its truth predicate, the least predicate closed under its constructors (`Exists/Proto/PropSorted.ev_mem_iff`). Instances, outside the default build, with no `sorry`: `List`, `Box`, `Duo`, `Rose`, and six harder nested and value-indexed declarations.

**The decomposition** (`DataModel/Exists/Decomposition.lean`; each piece believed true by its author): (1) `runT_congrDM`, the run reads a model only at the declarations of `K.Γ`, stated; (2) the same with `Γ` narrowed to the declarations a term can reach; (3) the canonical field type of an accepted declaration, an interpreter over the first-order fragment, and its agreement with the run; (4) guardedness of it from the checker's nesting rule; (5) `Prop`-sorted declarations in general; (6) the records' parameter clauses; (7) parameter types; (8) a fact about the checker alone: a declaration's field-type decisions do not change when Σ grows past it; (9) the assembly. Riskiest: (3), at a type function returning data and at equations keyed by small readings, and (4), against main's D107 as landed. None is proved.

**The positivity finding** (D85): fixed on main as D107 (the nestable parameters are a greatest fixed point, own occurrences included). `Exists/Positivity.lean` holds the condition the existence proof needs and the battery, with a second, independent hole of the same kind; on this branch, which predates main's D107, they are `#guard`ed as accepted (outside the default build), and must be flipped when main is merged.

### D93. The drop at the join: decisions in force, what it rests on, and four findings for the checker (slice lane, 2026-11-20; `spine-slice` 10542690, part merged)

**Decisions in force.** The reference's `dropAt` tests no meaning: a place the shared copy decision does not call a copy is accessed and written `moved` whatever the model reads there, and nothing is done exactly where the write cannot be made (9da91a0c). It replaced a test for `⋆` that mirrored the checker's and agreed with it only given the typing of the whole state. `ShapeFacts` gained (C⋆), a place at the join's grain holding `⋆` is a copy, restated after its first form was false at a data field of a proof, and (Hη≠), no η-record names its own unknown. [Access] and [End] conclude the extended form (`size ∧ ∃ ρ' Δ', ExtOk ∧ Rel`), since an end's re-runs and its [Repack] make unknowns (19d86568). `substEnv_neutral_step` and `placeTypeQ_step` are fields of the induction hypothesis.

**Where it stands at 2afd418c.** `joinDrop_step` and `dropOne_step_asm` have no `sorry` in their bodies and rest on: `ctx_botWrite`, `recordedNeutral_ok`, `etaFresh_sim`, `Rel.kept`, `DecTab.back`, seventeen `grows_*` (D88), and `stepField_dat` (reopened by D99).
